2026-08-15T23:20:23.4347262Z ##[start-action display=Set Trivy environment variables;id=__aquasecurity_trivy-action.__run_3]
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4362522Z ##[group]Run # Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4363619Z [36;1m# Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4364329Z [36;1m# This limitation affects how we handle default values and empty inputs.[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4364906Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4365348Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4365748Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4366203Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4366421Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4366655Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4366935Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4367177Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4367405Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4367839Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4368829Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4369543Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4370345Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4370980Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4371351Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided() {[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4371647Z [36;1m  local var_name="$1"[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4371921Z [36;1m  local input_value="$2"[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4372204Z [36;1m  local default_value="$3"[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4372480Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4372802Z [36;1m  if [ -n "$input_value" ] && [ "$input_value" != "$default_value" ]; then[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4373332Z [36;1m    # If action was provided with explicit input by the caller set that[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4373898Z [36;1m    # Use printf %q to safely escape special characters and prevent command injection[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4374611Z [36;1m    printf 'export %s=%q\n' "$var_name" "$input_value" >> trivy_envs.txt[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4375008Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4375223Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4375425Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4375736Z [36;1m# Set environment variables, handling those with default values[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4376350Z [36;1m# cf. https://aquasecurity.github.io/trivy/latest/docs/configuration/#environment-variables[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4376934Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_INPUT" "$INPUT_INPUT" ""[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4377390Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_EXIT_CODE" "$INPUT_EXIT_CODE" ""[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4377918Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_IGNORE_UNFIXED" "$INPUT_IGNORE_UNFIXED" "false"[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4378687Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_PKG_TYPES" "$INPUT_VULN_TYPE" "os,library"[0m
2026-08-15T23:20:23.4379319Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_SEVERITY" "$INPUT_SEVERITY" "UNKNOWN,LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL"[0m

2026-08-15T23:28:15.9807713Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9808148Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9808856Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9809345Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9809567Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9809956Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9810417Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9810826Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9811206Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9811970Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9813307Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9814554Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9815985Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9817095Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9817483Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided() {[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9817792Z [36;1m  local var_name="$1"[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9818079Z [36;1m  local input_value="$2"[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9818367Z [36;1m  local default_value="$3"[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9818925Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9819265Z [36;1m  if [ -n "$input_value" ] && [ "$input_value" != "$default_value" ]; then[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9819807Z [36;1m    # If action was provided with explicit input by the caller set that[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9820583Z [36;1m    # Use printf %q to safely escape special characters and prevent command injection[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9821159Z [36;1m    printf 'export %s=%q\n' "$var_name" "$input_value" >> trivy_envs.txt[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9821558Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9821781Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9821991Z [36;1m[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9822314Z [36;1m# Set environment variables, handling those with default values[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9822937Z [36;1m# cf. https://aquasecurity.github.io/trivy/latest/docs/configuration/#environment-variables[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9823528Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_INPUT" "$INPUT_INPUT" ""[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9823987Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_EXIT_CODE" "$INPUT_EXIT_CODE" ""[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9824515Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_IGNORE_UNFIXED" "$INPUT_IGNORE_UNFIXED" "false"[0m

2026-08-15T23:28:15.9825715Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_SEVERITY" "$INPUT_SEVERITY" "UNKNOWN,LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL"[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9826307Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_FORMAT" "$INPUT_FORMAT" "table"[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9826786Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_TEMPLATE" "$INPUT_TEMPLATE" ""[0m
2026-08-15T23:28:15.9827249Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided "TRIVY_OUTPUT" "$INPUT_OUTPUT" ""[0m
